# 1995 — every flow · minute · incomplete · complete · trap

**Status:** RESEARCH LOCK. Do not implement until this year is named as a wave.
**Feel:** Win95 · you can buy a book · SSL
**Star:** SSL checkout · `itt95-ssl-checkout` · `sites/amazon/ssl-checkout.html`
**ILS June:** 23,500 · 44,838,900
**Leftover-official dests in this file:** 486
**Official 10:** frozen in `js/config/flow-trails.js`. **Guided 6:** frozen in `ui/year/start-data.js`.
**Law:** leftover never writes `itt95-ssl-checkout`. Trap / empty / 0 ticks / wrong pick / skip-wait never write. Neighbor-year keys stay empty.
**5k:** research envelope (ILS + Hosting.com + Pew + this dest list). Not 5,000 new rooms.

Open dests as `/years/1995/<href>`. Serve `python3 -m http.server 8080 --bind 127.0.0.1`.

---

## G0 · Guided 6 (frozen · not a 7th item)

Start at `years/1995/pages/home.html` (or the year shell Starting Point).
The painted `<ol>` is **exactly 6**. A 7th `<li>` is a fail.
1. About 1995 — Win95 · commercial Web
2. SSL checkout — name + card + city REAL
3. Amazon books → cart
4. AuctionWeb — bid higher (not eBay yet)
5. GeoCities homestead — claim a free page
6. Year flow map — full trail tree

Walk: About → ★ gold → official leftovers named in the list → map. Visiting a guided dest is not a write. Only the dest machine writes.

---

## G1 · ★ GOLD · `itt95-ssl-checkout`

**Room:** `years/1995/sites/amazon/ssl-checkout.html`
**Hook:** `data-ssl-form`
**Nostalgia:** SSL checkout — this is the year chip. One star.

**Incomplete never writes**
- Empty name / card / city · Allow-all trap never write.
- Leftover panel on this room (if any) writes a leftover key, **not** `itt95-ssl-checkout`.

**Complete writes**
- Name + card + city · honesty · Submit. Writes itt95-ssl-checkout.
- Payload is REAL (`real: true`) · year `1995` · key `itt95-ssl-checkout`.
- Reload still shows saved. Neighbor `itt-ssl-checkout` — do not write `itt94-*` or `itt96-*` gold.
- **Next after gold:** Bid higher · AuctionWeb → `years/1995/sites/auctionweb/item-laser.html`

**Do not:** second star · leftover save that stamps this key · dest-field plaque as the gold machine.

## L · Leftover-official — every dest · 486 flows

Protocol is the same machine (`js/immersion/leftover-official.js`). The minutes below are dest-true: key, needPick, field, next, hops.

### L-0001 · `itt95-altavista-about` · sites/altavista/about.html

**Open:** `/years/1995/sites/altavista/about.html`
**Panel:** `[data-lo-panel]:has([data-lo-save][data-lo-key="altavista-about"])`
**Need:** field ≥2 (placeholder “query leftover”)
**Nostalgia hop targets from here:** `yahoo` · `amazon` · `auctionweb`

**Incomplete never writes**
1. Wait until `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="altavista-about"]` has `data-lo-bound="1"`.
2. Clear `localStorage['itt95-altavista-about']`.
3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.
6. Ticks + pick (if any) + empty field + Save → “Empty never writes.”
7. `itt95-ssl-checkout` stays empty. `itt94-altavista-about` and `itt96-altavista-about` stay empty.

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).
- Type ≥2 chars in `[data-lo-field]` (e.g. “query leftover”). Empty never writes.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="altavista-about"]`.
- `localStorage['itt95-altavista-about']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"1995", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt95-altavista-about`.
- Does **not** write `itt95-ssl-checkout`.
- **Next:** Next leftover → `/years/1995/sites/espn/more.html`
